A grown-up-sized long-sleeved, ankle-length, hooded zip-front novelty costume onesie in brushed fleece, made as a mecha pilot in a flight suit costume. From head to toe the onesie is the mecha pilot in a flight suit, with the hood forms the top or head of the mecha pilot in a flight suit and the body carries its main shape, colours and markings. The design follows a padded suit with armour pads, but as a cosy plush onesie. The hood adds a helmet. The feet are made as boots. Nothing is added that a mecha pilot in a flight suit would not have; it is one soft, simple, instantly readable mecha pilot in a flight suit onesie. Novelty embellishments, glitter-flecked tulle layered at the hem, quilted puffy panels on the chest and chunky pom-pom trim along the hood edge and cuffs. It reads as a joyful, exaggerated novelty onesie, stuffed with texture and detail.
A grown-up-sized short-sleeved, long-legged, hooded zip-front novelty costume onesie in plush minky, made as a phoenix costume. The whole onesie is shaped and coloured as the phoenix itself, with the body is the phoenix, with its true shape, colours and surface details sewn on in padded fabric. It takes a feathered flame costume and makes it a squishy wearable onesie. The hood adds a feathered crest. Nothing is added that a phoenix would not have; it is one soft, simple, instantly readable phoenix onesie. Novelty embellishments, soft chenille patches, rows of sequins sewn in swirling patterns and a stuffed 3D accent piece that sticks out. Everything is chosen to be fun to look at and tactile to feel, bumpy, fluffy, shiny and soft in turn.
A roomy adult-sized long-sleeved, ankle-length, hooded zip-front novelty costume onesie in quilted jersey, made as a mecha pilot in a flight suit costume. It is a plush novelty costume of the mecha pilot in a flight suit, with the mecha pilot in a flight suit's recognisable parts, colours and textures built from layered fabric, filling the whole suit. The costume idea is a padded suit with armour pads, turned into a soft onesie. The hood adds a helmet. The feet are made as boots. It reads from across a room as a mecha pilot in a flight suit, with large simple shapes, clear colours and exaggerated, funny proportions. Novelty embellishments, glitter-flecked tulle layered at the hem, a stuffed 3D accent piece that sticks out and oversized novelty buttons in contrasting colours. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.

The attached hood is sewn as a brass face plate in velvet. The suit is footless, so the footwear is left off. The sleeves end in plain finished cuffs. A fabric version of key is stitched onto the suit. It is tailored like a real garment, with proper seams, darts, hems and stitching lines, entirely in the outfit's own colours and cloths. The outfit is stretched to the garment's own straight-sided shape and fills it completely, so it suits any body size. It is a complete costume of clockwork automaton in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a brass-plated chest with gears, a brass face plate (as the hood), boots (as the feet), a key sewn or attached to the suit. The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, enamel-style pins and patches, knitted rib panels with raised cable texture and oversized novelty buttons in contrasting colours. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.

The attached hood is sewn as a braided crown in velour. The suit is footless, so the footwear is left off. The attached gloves are sewn from fabric in the outfit's trim colours. The garment is assembled from cut panels of fabric joined with visible seams, topstitching and piping, and it hangs as one coherent piece with every part in the outfit's own cloth. The outfit is stretched to the garment's own straight-sided shape and fills it completely, so it suits any body size. It is a complete costume of space opera noble in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a long silver gown with a sash, a braided crown (as the hood), slippers (as the feet). The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, oversized novelty buttons in contrasting colours, braided cord drawstrings with knotted ends and knitted rib panels with raised cable texture. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.
